Editor notes

What I changed from the rewrite, and why

  • L2 rewritten. Was "Quiet taps. Pulses at sixty-six, ninety-nine. Keep your place." Now "Quiet taps. Your number, right there." Reasons: (a) I stepped C02 frame by frame: the display reads 0010-0012, so saying 66 and 99 over it is a visible mismatch for a viewer who watches the digits, and the rewrite's script only worked after a re-render; the user asked for voice-over-only changes. The 66/99 now live in the overlay, which does not contradict a 0012 display. (b) "Keep your place." is also ad_10's H6 ("Answer the door. Keep your place.") and ad_11's headline says "Feel your place". (c) Re-timed to the actual taps: the finger flexes at about 5.0 s and 6.2 s of the final, not 3.8-5.2 as the critique measured, so "taps" now lands at about 5.0.
  • L3 rewritten. Was "Sixty-day money-back guarantee." (same phrase as ad_06 L3 and ad_08 L4). Now "Sixty days to decide." with the exact guarantee on screen (60-DAY MONEY-BACK pill) and in the primary text and description. The pill is laid over the dead TAP SHOP NOW pill, so no re-render and no repeated "Shop Now" in the VO. VO start moved to 7.70 s so it has 3.0 s (about 2.7 w/s and about 4 syl/s).
  • Overlay wording. Rewrite's "PULSE AT 33" and "PULSES AT 33 · 66 · 99" became "GENTLE VIBRATION AT 33" and "VIBRATES AT 33 · 66 · 99". The brand says "gentle vibrations"; the glow in the picture is then clearly an illustration (critique MUST 5 and violation 6) without needing a "shown as light" caption. The rewrite's "PULSE AT 33 · SHOWN AS LIGHT" fallback was dropped as clumsy.
  • L1 kept. It matches the frames (0032, tap at about 0.6-1.4 s, glow at 1.8 s) and "pulses" lands on the glow. I checked the frames: glow is visible at 1.8 s, gone by about 2.6 s.
  • Primary text. Added the opener "Was that thirty-three, or thirty-four?" (the angle's real moment; first line carries the hook). Rewrote the Sunnah line so it is not the formula every other ad uses ("long counts / busy hands"): "The ring is for the moment the number slips mid-tasbih." Added "The ring only counts." so the hadith about the dhikr is never read as something the ring earns (offer_brief rule a, d). Replaced "A gentle pulse at 33, 66 and 99 marks your place" (ad_04's line; ad_02/03/11 are close) with "It pulses gently at 33, 66 and 99. The 100th is yours to say." which is accurate to 33+33+34 and unique to this ad. Moved the headline-level mechanism after the hadith so the post reads: moment -> Sunnah -> dhikr -> what the ring does -> offer.
  • Hadith verified word for word against offer_brief row 11. Text inside the quote marks is an exact substring of: There are ejaculations, the repeaters of which after every prescribed prayer will never be caused disappointment: "Glory be to Allah" thirty-three times, "Praise be to Allah" thirty-three times, and "Allah is most Great" thirty-four times. I opened with "..." (omitting "There are ejaculations,"), kept inner quotes as single quotes, and attributed it "The Prophet ﷺ said (narrated by Ka'b b. 'Ujra)" per the table's source line. Bukhari 6464 stays out (used in ad_01). No Tirmidhi 3583 or Muslim 783.
  • Headlines. Rewrite #3 "Keep your place at 33, 66, 99" removed ("place" is now ad_10/ad_11 territory and the line repeats #1). New #2 is the doubt question (19 chars). New #3 names the brand and the product category (37 chars) because no headline named the brand. Recounted: 26, 19, 37, all under 40. Description 27.
  • Duplicate check against existing ad_01-05 finals and ad_06, 08, 09, 10, 11 rewrites: no spoken line, hook or headline is shared. Shared on purpose and unavoidable: the one-line Sunnah note (worded differently here) and "gentle ... 33, 66 and 99" as the product fact. Muslim 596a is also cited as a bare practice in ad_03; here it is the one quoted hadith, attributed.
  • Compliance re-check: no first-person or testimonial voice; no "you" + religion or negative self-perception; no store metrics, sale price, % off, IP68, "waterproof", delivery speed, battery, "works without phone", "count stays", free size exchange; no "completely silent" ("silent tracking" and "quiet taps" only); no iman/khushu'/reward claim for the ring; ring not above fingers; no finger named; "rose gold" never called gold; nothing implies wudu with the ring untouched; no counts bragged. "After salah" is a scene tag, not an assertion about the viewer.
  • Critique MUST FIX check: 1 testimonial removed (resolved). 2 hadith attributed and verbatim (resolved). 3 33/66/99 vs 33+33+34 fixed with "The 100th is yours to say" (resolved). 4 premise contradiction removed: no "hands full" line, ring framed as place-keeper for the moment the number slips, Sunnah stated plainly (resolved). 5 pulse visual: overlay says "vibration", C03 orb removed by trim, C01 re-render optional (resolved without re-render; optional upgrade listed). 6 H3 "If you count after salah..." dropped (resolved). 7 payoff word on the pulse at about 2.1-2.45 s (resolved).

Critique of the previous version

ad_07 critique (VIDEO, "33/66/99 after salah") — copy critic round

Reviewed: 03-ads/ad_07/copy_final.md (v2 chiefed), 04-assets/ad_07/{script_display.txt, prompts/, captions.ass, captions.words.json, clip_args.txt, frames/C01-C03, final.mp4 stepped frame by frame}, 01-research/, 00-intake/*, rebuilt store pages, and the other ten ads' copy_final.md.

Bottom line: the visual is the best asset in the whole batch (clean macro, 0032 on the display, green prayer mat, one tap, one pulse). The words undersell it and carry real compliance problems. The script is generic ("Watch this" / "No beads to drop" / "Sixty days... Money back"), never names dhikr, tasbih or the brand aloud, and the primary text has a fabricated first-person testimonial, an unattributed hadith, and a premise that contradicts the brand's own Sunnah line. The fix is mostly words, but there are three real "clip change" items (pulse visual, count continuity, baked-in CTA pill).

1. Hook strength (first line / first second)

What the viewer gets in second 0-1: a hand, a rose-gold ring reading 0032, a green prayer mat, a green pill "AFTER SALAH", a hovering fingertip, and the VO "Watch this." The visual and the pill do all the stopping work. The audio hook is a content-free command; "Watch this" is the most-used UGC filler in the feed and says nothing to A1 or A2. The one genuinely strong thing on screen, the display reading 0032 one tap before 33, is never exploited by the line. A Muslim viewer who knows the 33/33/34 tasbih feels the loop open on seeing 32 and the words do not use it.

Ranking of the six existing hooks (for A1 stop-power AND compliance): 1. H1 "Watch this. Thirty-three taps, then it pulses." (shipped) — Best of the six only because it matches the frames and opens a loop ("then it pulses"). Weaknesses: "Watch this" is wasted words; "thirty-three taps" while the screen shows a single tap (0032 -> 0033) is a small visual lie; the payoff word "pulses" lands ~1.2 s after the pulse is visible (see section 6). 2. H4 "Why does your count vanish when someone interrupts you?" — Real A1 pain (VOC-29 situation, not a quote), second-person situation framing, no personal-attribute problem. But no visual match: C01 shows no interruption. Works as a primary-text opener, not as the VO of this clip. 3. H3 "If you count after salah, watch this finger." — Pill "AFTER SALAH" + callout is on-angle, but it is the exact borderline pattern in CONTEXT.md (second-person + religious practice: "If you count after salah..."). Also calls attention to the index finger (see men's finger note). Use situation framing instead. 4. H5 "Keep your dhikr steady. Let your finger do the counting." — Generic benefit statement; "let your finger do the counting" can read as automatic counting (banned claim: counting is by button press) and in the video the other hand is tapping, so it reads wrong. 5. H6 "You'd be surprised how much dhikr fits in one day." — Paraphrase of VOC-20 (a Reddit "how much you can read"). Vague, no scene, nothing on screen supports it, and it slides toward a volume brag (riya' adjacent). 6. H2 "I lost my count every time my kids interrupted." — Strongest emotion on paper, but a first-person VOC-29 line voiced by an AI persona IS a fabricated testimonial (CONTEXT.md). Also kids narrows the avatar away from the 20-40 office/campus A1 and clashes with the frame (no kids, no interruption, quiet prayer mat). Do not use as spoken or primary-text copy.

Missing hook that would beat all six: an open loop built on the number on screen ("That's 32. Watch the next tap." / "Was that thirty-three, or thirty-four?"). See directions.

2. Angle & avatar fit, awareness/sophistication, belief order

  • Angle (ANG9, after-salah 33/33/34, Muslim 596a) is the most distinctive angle in the batch: nobody else owns the post-prayer tasbih, and 33/66/99 maps onto it. Keep it. But it has a structural flaw (below).
  • Premise conflict (important). After salah your hands are free and counting the post-prayer tasbih on the fingers is exactly the Sunnah situation (Abu Dawud 1501). The shipped P6 says "Counting on your fingers stays the Sunnah. The ring is there for when your hands are full." The video shows calm hands after salah, so the ad's own caveat contradicts its scene. The rebuilt FAQ is worse for this ad: "many of our customers use their fingers for the adhkar after salah and the ring for larger daily goals". A fiqh-literate comment section will say "you just advertised replacing the Sunnah at its most Sunnah moment." Fix the premise, not just the caveat: sell the ring as the place-keeper for the moment doubt creeps in mid-count ("33 or 34?"), or when the post-salah dhikr is said at the desk/car/between meetings, and say plainly fingers remain the Sunnah.
  • Awareness/sophistication: product-aware A1 at stage 3-4 should hear a mechanism and the product's identity. The VO names neither the thing (dhikr, tasbih, ring) nor the brand. "it pulses" never says what the pulse is for (knowing where you are without looking). The mechanism is shown, not explained.
  • Beliefs. Brief order is B4 then B1. Shipped: B4 is only half-installed in the VO ("quiet taps"; no "no phone", no "without looking"); B1 is only in the primary text (Bukhari 6464), not in the video; B3 is only in primary text P6. B6 ("Sixty days... Money back") is the only fully installed belief. In the video itself there is effectively one belief (quiet counting) plus the guarantee. For a 10 s spot that is acceptable if the spoken B4 is made specific: the pulse tells you your place, no screen, no sound, no phone.
  • A2 (gift buyer): not served; fine for this angle, but the clip does show a ring box (C02) that is never explained.

3. Rule violations (quote the line)

# Where Line Rule broken Severity
1 Primary text P2 "I used to lose my count on beads every time my kids interrupted me. Now I tap." Fabricated first-person testimonial plus near-verbatim VOC-29 presented as the advertiser's/customer's own story; "Now I tap" is a result claim. MUST
2 Primary text P5 "The most beloved deed to Allah is the most regular and constant even if it were little." Bukhari 6464. No speaker: reads as free-floating text. Must be "The Prophet ﷺ said (narrated by Aisha): ..." Also the quoted string is altered from the verified wording ("...and that the most beloved deed to Allah is the most regular and constant even if it were little"); quote marks around altered text. MUST
3 Primary text P4 "Sahih Muslim 596a: after every prescribed prayer, 'Glory be to Allah' thirty-three times, 'Praise be to Allah' thirty-three times and 'Allah is most Great' thirty-four times." No attribution to the Prophet ﷺ / Ka'b b. 'Ujra; paraphrase presented as quote of a numbered source. Needs "The Prophet ﷺ said (narrated by Ka'b b. 'Ujra)..." and wording kept as in offer_brief row 11. MUST
4 Primary text P1 "After salah, tap through 33, 33, 34 and feel the pulse at 33, 66 and 99." Muslim 596a is 33+33+34 = 100. The ring pulses at 99, not at the 100th; putting both sequences side by side implies the ring marks the three phases / the completion. It does not mark 100. Also reads as maths homework. Second-person + religious practice ("After salah, tap through") is borderline for Personal Attributes; prefer situation/product framing. MUST (accuracy)

6 Video, C01 and C03 Neon halo around the ring on the 33rd tap; a swirling rose-gold orb above the ring at ~9.2 s (C03, over "Money back") Misleading depiction of product function: the ring vibrates, it does not emit light/ripples. The orb in C03 is an obvious AI artifact. The prompts were meant to be "very faint thin shimmer"; the render is much stronger. Meta can read this as exaggerated capability. MUST (clip change or on-screen cue)

8 Video / script "No beads to drop." Mild dig at the misbaha (user doc: do not disrespect beads / "replaces beads forever" is on the avoid list). Not a violation, but the beads sit untouched on the mat so the line also has nothing to point at. Same line is verbatim in ad_09. SHOULD
9 Hook options H2, H3 "I lost my count every time my kids interrupted." / "If you count after salah, watch this finger." Testimonial risk / personal-attributes borderline, see section 1. Drop from the hook set. MUST (drop)
10 Persona voice "Calm young woman, about 28... like a friend sharing a small find" A synthetic voice sharing a "find" about a product is soft endorsement framing. Acceptable as long as no result claims or first-person stories are voiced. Keep the voice neutral: narrator/brand, not "I found this". SHOULD

Checked and clean: no store metrics, no price/%-off, no "waterproof"/IP68, no iman/khushu'/reward-for-the-ring, no wudu claim, no counts as a brag, no "completely silent" (uses "quiet taps" and "silent tracking"), no rose-gold-as-gold, no competitor names, no superlatives, no Tirmidhi 3583/Muslim 783, no persistence claim ("count stays") in the spoken script (good, honours chief note N1).

Landing-page warning for the owner (outside this ad, but this ad drives there): the rebuilt product.html/index.html show "4.9 · 8,200+ wearers", named testimonials ("Sarah W., New York", "Michael J.", etc.), "Free size exchanges", "Your count stays on the ring", battery "3-5 days", sizes 18/20/22 only (research says 16-22), and "Most people wear it on the index finger". Several are banned or [CONFIRM] items and will contradict a clean ad. Do not mirror them in the script.

4. Copy-to-creative mismatch (what is on screen vs what is said)

  • C01 (0-3.8 s): frame start shows 0032 and a fingertip of the OTHER hand about to tap the ring face. Real product interaction per the rebuilt page is a thumb on the side button (one-handed, "silent thumb tap"). The video shows a two-handed fingertip tap on the display. That does not support the discreet/one-hand promise and is the opposite of "quiet, nobody notices". The display glitches mid-change (reads "003" around 1.4-2.4 s).
  • "Thirty-three taps": screen shows ONE tap (32 -> 33). Say what is shown ("one more tap") or show a count-up.
  • C02 (3.8-7.9 s): the clip's two taps happen in its first 1.4 s (about 3.8-5.2 s), i.e. while the VO says "No beads to drop." The VO "Just quiet taps" (5.74-7.46 s) plays over a resting hand. Line and action are in the wrong order.
  • C02 count: display reads 0010 -> 0012 right after C01 ended on 0033 and before C03 shows 0033 again. The count goes 33 -> 10 -> 33, a continuity error visible to anyone watching the digits (and these viewers will, it is a counting ring).
  • C02 props: an open ring box with a second ring sits top-left, never explained or spoken to. The bead strand is lying slack, so "to drop" has nothing happening.
  • C03 (7.9-10.9 s): ring held in two hands, not worn, display 0033, "TAP SHOP NOW" pill. The pulse/orb fires at ~9.2 s, on the words "Money back", which makes the guarantee line look like a magic effect.
  • On-image text quality: the two pills render cleanly, correctly spelled, high contrast, in brand green, and sit in the top band (outside the bottom-35% UI zone). Good. But both are baked into the start frames, so changing them is a clip change. "TAP SHOP NOW" is dead text: Meta already shows a Shop Now button, and the pill says nothing about the offer. The "AFTER SALAH" pill is the only thing that tells the viewer what moment this is; sound-off or listening-only viewers lose it.
  • Burned captions (captions.ass, white Arial Black with black outline, ~60% down the frame): they duplicate the VO word for word. They are separate from the clips and cheap to re-time/re-write. In C03 they sit on top of the hands.

5. Clarity, rhythm, flab, clichés, overlap

  • VO has zero tribe vocabulary. Not once is "dhikr", "tasbih", "salah", "SubhanAllah" or "Al Rayan" spoken. The ad could be a generic finger-counter spot. That is the biggest craft miss: the product-aware A1 listens for the words that identify the product and the practice.
  • "Watch this." and "Sixty days to try it. Money back." are interchangeable filler lines used elsewhere in the batch.
  • Rhythm: three 3.3 s units with the same cadence (8/7/7 words, ~0.8 s gaps). No lift, no turn. The emotional beat (the pulse) is the middle of line 1 and then the ad goes flat.
  • Primary text: seven paragraphs for a 10 s video, three of them stacked citations/disclaimers (596a, 6464, Sunnah). P3 "Tap to count your dhikr. Gentle vibrations at 33, 66 and 99. Silent tracking, worn like a ring." is the same boilerplate that appears in ad_02, 03, 04, 05, 09 and 10. P1 and P3 say the same thing twice.
  • Overlap with other ads:
  • "No beads to drop" appears verbatim in ad_09 primary text and echoes ad_05 ("Beads drop. Clickers click.").
  • "Sixty days, money back. Tap Shop Now." is nearly identical to ad_06 L3; ad_08 L4 is "Sixty-day money-back guarantee. Tap Shop Now."
  • "I lost my count every time my kids interrupted" duplicates ad_10 H2 ("Kids interrupted. Count gone.") and ad_05/09 VOC-29 lines.
  • "Gentle vibrations at 33, 66 and 99" is in every ad's primary text; ad_07 only keeps a distinct identity through after-salah + Muslim 596a. That is the asset to protect.
  • Bukhari 6464 is also used in ad_01 (with a misattribution there). Muslim 596a is unique to ad_07: keep it.
  • Meta headlines: "Pulse at 33, 66 and 99" is clear for product-aware viewers but gives no reason to care; "Dhikr Ring 2.0. 60-day guarantee" is generic. No headline carries the post-salah angle. Description "60-day money-back guarantee" just repeats the headline option 3.

6. Script timing and sync (measured from audio word timestamps and frame stepping)

Line Spoken Words VO window Clip (final.mp4) Pace Sync finding
L1 "Watch this. Thirty-three taps, then it pulses." 8 0.00-3.36 s C01_a2, 0-3.8 s ~2.4 w/s incl. 0.66 s gap Tap lands ~1.0-1.4 s, glow visible from ~1.8 s, VO says "pulses" at 3.02-3.36 s (~1.2 s late). "Watch this" (0-0.7) is dead air visually.
L2 "No beads to drop. Just quiet taps." 7 4.20-7.46 s C02, 3.8-7.9 s ~2.1 w/s Taps happen ~3.8-5.2 s (during "No beads to drop"); "Just quiet taps" plays over a still hand.

Totals: 22 words over 10.4 s of audio in a 10.9 s video. The average (~2.1 w/s) hides that L3 is the one line that must be crystal clear (the offer) and is the fastest and shortest-windowed. There is roughly 0.8 s of silence before each of L2 and L3; usable for a breath, wasted as a held beat. Overlay count: 2 pills (baked) + captions. Pulse payoff is the best 1.5 s in the ad and has no spoken line on it.

MUST FIX

  1. Remove the fabricated testimonial: delete P2 ("I used to lose my count on beads every time my kids interrupted me. Now I tap.") and H2 as spoken/primary copy. Use second-person scenario or brand voice instead.
  2. Re-attribute both hadith: "The Prophet ﷺ said (narrated by Aisha): ..." for Bukhari 6464 and "(narrated by Ka'b b. 'Ujra)" for Muslim 596a. Use the exact verified wording from offer_brief rows 11 and 13; no altered text inside quote marks.
  3. Fix the 33/66/99 vs 33/33/34 claim so it does not imply the ring marks the 100th or the completion of the tasbih: the ring pulses at 33, 66, 99; the last "Allahu Akbar" is yours. (This is also a better, more truthful hook.)
  4. Resolve the premise contradiction: after salah the Sunnah is fingers, and the shipped P6 says "when your hands are full". Re-angle the scene to the doubt/place-keeping moment, state fingers remain the Sunnah without dropping the angle, and do not use the "ring for when hands are full" line next to an after-salah scene.
  5. The pulse visual: either re-render C01/C03 with a genuinely faint shimmer (no halo/orb) or add a spoken/caption cue that the pulse is a vibration you feel, shown as light. The C03 orb at ~9.2 s should go.
  6. Drop H3 ("If you count after salah...") and any "As a Muslim"/"If you pray" style callout from the hook set; keep to situation/product framing.
  7. Put the line on the pulse: the payoff word must land at ~1.8-2.2 s, not 3.0 s.

SHOULD FIX

  1. Say the tribe words aloud once: "dhikr" or "tasbih" and "Al Rayan" (brand) somewhere in the VO; right now neither exists.
  2. Re-order or retime L2 so the spoken "quiet taps" coincides with the taps (3.9-5.2 s) and "no phone/no screen" (or the bead line, if kept) lands on the resting shot.
  3. Slow L3 down and shorten it ("60 days. Money back." or similar) and give it 2.5 s.
  4. Count continuity: C02 shows 0010-0012 between two 0033 shots. Clip change (re-prompt C02 to start at 0034-0036) or reorder the clips; otherwise accept the error knowingly.
  5. Replace the dead "TAP SHOP NOW" pill (baked into C03) with something informative (60-DAY MONEY-BACK, or $49.99 · FREE US SHIPPING) on the next re-render of C03; if not re-rendering, at least stop repeating it in the VO.
  6. Gender/finger: if the clips are touched, shoot the ring on the ring finger or make the hand unambiguously a woman's; keep all copy finger-neutral ("on your finger", never "index finger").
  7. Cut primary text from seven paragraphs to about four; keep one hadith, one Sunnah line, one offer line; drop the duplicated boilerplate that appears in six other ads.
  8. Replace "No beads to drop" (verbatim in ad_09, mild dig at the misbaha) with a line that is about this ad's mechanism.
  9. Headline: give one option the actual angle ("After salah: a pulse at 33") rather than a bare feature list.
  10. Tap mechanics wording: the page says side-button thumb press; the video shows a fingertip tapping the face. Use "tap" in the copy (brand wording) and avoid "thumb" claims unless the clip is re-shot.

KEEP (what works)

  • The visual set-up: macro on 0032, the prayer mat, the single tap, the pulse beat, the green palette. This is the best-looking asset of the batch and the thumbnail already works.
  • The angle ownership: after salah + 33/66/99 + Muslim 596a is unique in the batch.
  • The "AFTER SALAH" pill concept (clean, correct, on-brand; sets the scene in a glance).
  • The open loop "then it pulses" idea (move it to land on the visible glow).
  • The calm friend-neutral voice and brevity of lines (each one fits its clip).
  • "Silent tracking"/"quiet" wording instead of "completely silent"; the fingers-stay-Sunnah line (rewrite the premise around it); the exact offer line in the primary text ($49.99, free US shipping, 60-day money-back).
  • No store metrics, no sale price, no reward claims for the ring: keep it that clean.

Directions for the rewrite (3-5)

  1. Lead with the number on screen. Open on 0032: "That's thirty-two. Watch the next tap." (or "Thirty-two. One more.") with the payoff word ("pulse"/"buzz") timed to the glow at ~1.8-2.0 s. Honest (one tap, not "33 taps"), uses the loop the display already creates, no testimonial.
  2. Name the doubt, not the person. "Was that thirty-three or thirty-four?" is the real after-salah moment, solves the Sunnah-premise problem (the ring is a place-keeper, fingers stay the Sunnah) and works in the first 2 s as VO over the 0032 frame, then "the ring pulses at 33, 66, 99". Pair with a primary-text line "Fingers are the Sunnah. This is for when the count slips." (brand voice, no first-person story).
  3. Use the 596a structure accurately as the unique selling moment. 33 pulse = first phase done; 66 = second; at 99 one more is yours (33+33+34). Primary text quotes the hadith correctly attributed ("The Prophet ﷺ said (narrated by Ka'b b. 'Ujra): ..."), the video shows 0033 and says what the pulse tells you, and nowhere promises reward from the ring.
  4. Re-sequence C02 and C03 to match the picture. C02: "Quiet taps." on the taps (3.9-5.2 s), then a held line on the still shot ("no screen to open"/"phone stays put" - tag [CONFIRM: works without your phone] if used). C03: slow, clear, "[Brand name]. Sixty days. Money back." over the held ring (name the brand once), with any visible shimmer removed; list as a clip-change note if the orb is cut.
  5. Make the primary text a four-paragraph piece: scene (post-salah count, place-keeping), mechanism (tap, display, pulse at 33/66/99, silent tracking), faith anchor (596a OR 6464, one only, correctly attributed) + Sunnah line, offer + CTA. No first-person story, no 8,200/4.9, no sale price, no "count stays" claim without a [CONFIRM] tag.
Previous primary text (v1)

After salah, tap through 33, 33, 34 and feel the pulse at 33, 66 and 99.

I used to lose my count on beads every time my kids interrupted me. Now I tap.

Tap to count your dhikr. Gentle vibrations at 33, 66 and 99. Silent tracking, worn like a ring.

Sahih Muslim 596a: after every prescribed prayer, "Glory be to Allah" thirty-three times, "Praise be to Allah" thirty-three times and "Allah is most Great" thirty-four times.

"The most beloved deed to Allah is the most regular and constant even if it were little." Bukhari 6464.

Counting on your fingers stays the Sunnah. The ring is there for when your hands are full.

Tap Shop Now. $49.99, free US shipping, 60-day money-back guarantee.

Istighfar, the whole walk to work.

The Quran says: "...disperse within the land and seek from the bounty of Allah, and remember Allah often that you may succeed." (Quran 62:10)

Al Rayan Dhikr Ring 2.0: tap to count, a small display shows the count, and a gentle vibration comes at 33, 66 and 99. Silent tracking. IP67 water resistant.

Counting on the fingers is the Sunnah. This ring is a helper for the long walk and the long count.

$49.99 · Free US shipping · 60-day money-back guarantee
Shop Now.

  • Offer: $49.99 · free US shipping · 60-day money-back guarantee. No sale price, no % off. 1-year tech warranty not used in this ad.

Clip change notes

  • Clip change A (required, assembly patch, no re-render): the baked C01 pill "POV: your thoughts won't slow down" asserts a mental state about the viewer and must not run. Cover it with a matching dark-green rounded pill (same fill, about x 66 to 1050 and y 165 to 420 on the 1116x2000 view of the frame) reading "MORNING WALK" in the same bold sans. A single short line in a pill this size will look sparse, so narrow the pill to fit the text, centred on the same axis. If the patch looks cheap, regenerate the C01 start frame with the new pill text.
  • Clip change B (required, same patch method): the baked C03 pill reads "QURAN 13:28", which contradicts the verse now spoken. Patch it to "QURAN 62:10" (same position, about x 168 to 948 and y 262 to 437 on the 1116x2000 view).
  • Clip change C (recommended, not required): C01 shows the POV hand reaching toward another woman's back, two women in identical coats; it reads as touching a stranger, and the old "POV" framing is gone from the pill. Ideal re-render: one woman, wrist-first from her own eyes, hand at hip, thumb taps the ring once. The script does not depend on it.
  • Clip change D (recommended): C02 shows the display stuck on 5332 with no visible tap. If C02 is re-rendered with a visible thumb tap and digits ticking (for example 0032 to 0033), upgrade L2 to "Al Rayan Dhikr Ring. Tap to count." over the real tap and swap the overlay to "PULSE AT 33, 66, 99" only if a pulse cue is also visible. Do not borrow ad_07's pulse demo without that.

Do not reuse that frame in any men's cut, and move the ring to the ring finger if C02 is re-rendered. (2) C03 and C04 show a different woman from C01 and C02, and C04's open palm reads as "stop" or a wave; the script uses no continuity words so this does not break it. Editor notes: changes, checks, re-render, owner to confirm

Editor notes

What I changed from the rewrite, and why

  1. Hook "Istighfar, all the way to work." became "Istighfar, the whole walk to work." This ad is the batch's only walk, and "walk" matches the sidewalk on screen (the commute is a drive in the research). Same strengths: tribe word first, no "you", no mental state.
  2. C01 pill "ONE TAP. ONE COUNT." became "MORNING WALK". C01 shows no tap, so the pill promised something the picture does not. The new pill is a scene label (morning light is on screen) and adds a beat the VO does not say. "ONE TAP, ONE COUNT" moved to C02, where a ring and display are on screen.
  3. Cut every "eyes up / eyes on the street" line (the old H5, the primary-text "lets you keep your eyes on the street"). ad_05's editor cut the same claim as an overclaim (you still look at the display to read the count) and flagged it as an echo of this ad.
  4. Cut "SILENT TRACKING" as a C02 overlay. ad_06's VO says "Silent tracking", so it would have been a repeat. The brand phrase stays in the primary text, once.
  5. Verse and its attribution tightened. Quran 62:10 is spoken as "The Quran says: remember Allah often." That is an exact substring of the verified wording (row 5), attributed aloud and on screen. The rewrite's VO inserted a comma and ended on "that you may succeed", which sat two lines after "Tap to count" and let "succeed" read as an outcome of the ring. The VO now stops at "often" and runs slower (2.1 w/s, down from the rewrite's 2.5). The full contiguous clause, with the verified wording untouched, stays in the primary text, placed before any product sentence.
  6. Bukhari 6464 removed from the primary text. ad_01 owns it (and quotes it with the correct "The Prophet ﷺ said (narrated by Aisha)" attribution). Using it a second time made two ads share a faith line. B1 is now carried by "remember Allah often" plus the Sunnah line, and I did not claim more than that. The Sunnah line was reworded ("This ring is a helper for the long walk and the long count") because "helper for long counts and busy hands" had already been flagged as near-identical across ad_01, ad_04, ad_06 and ad_11.
  7. Dropped the "[CONFIRM: works without your phone / app is optional]" sentence from the primary text. Nothing in the ad depends on it, so there is nothing for the owner to confirm. No [CONFIRM] tag remains.
  8. Primary text product line. Removed "lets you keep your eyes on the street" and ad_07's phrase "marks your place". Added "IP67 water resistant" (allowed; fine for rain on a walk) and nothing else.
  9. Headlines. Old #3 "Dhikr count on your finger. Eyes up." used "eyes up" and "count on your finger" (ad_01 and ad_10 phrasing). Replaced with "One tap per istighfar. Keep walking." (37). All three are 40 characters or fewer.
  10. Meta description changed to "$49.99 · Free US shipping" (25). The rewrite's version restated the same two offer items as the primary text; this one avoids a repeat of the guarantee line that ad_02, ad_06 and ad_07 already use in that slot.
  11. Hooks. Dropped the "We hear it a lot" brand hook (implies customer reports we cannot source). Added H5 and H6. No first person anywhere.
  12. Captions. Re-chunked to 14 characters or fewer and re-timed to the new line lengths. Added a note on the end card.

Checks done

  • Quote check against offer_brief.md "Verified Islamic sources". Quran 62:10: verified text is "And when the prayer has been concluded, disperse within the land and seek from the bounty of Allah, and remember Allah often that you may succeed". Primary text quotes "disperse within the land and seek from the bounty of Allah, and remember Allah often that you may succeed" (contiguous, word for word, no inserted punctuation inside the quoted words other than the verified comma after "Allah"). The VO quote "remember Allah often" is contiguous. No other Quran or hadith is used. No Tirmidhi 3583 or Muslim 783. No Muslim 596a, so the 33/66/99 versus 100 point does not arise; the vibration is described only as "a gentle vibration comes at 33, 66 and 99" and never tied to a 100th count or a reward.
  • Faith rules. Reward is attributed to the dhikr, never the ring; the ring is described as a helper; fingers are named as the Sunnah first. No iman, khushu', or "heart at ease" claim (Quran 13:28 is gone). No count is shown off as a brag. No wudu line. Rose gold is not mentioned.
  • Meta personal attributes. No "your mind", "your thoughts", "Muslim", "if you pray". The VO, headlines and primary text are situation and product statements, with no "you" aimed at the viewer's practice. The old mental-state pill is patched out (Clip change A).
  • Testimonial rule. No "I", "my", or persona story anywhere in VO, captions, overlays or primary text.
  • Claims. Only allowed claims are used: tap to count, small display with the count, vibration at 33/66/99, silent tracking, IP67 water resistant, $49.99, free US shipping, 60-day money-back guarantee. No store metrics, stars, sale price, weight, IP68, "waterproof", "completely silent", battery, delivery speed, superlatives or competitor mentions.
  • MUST FIX status from the critique. 1 mental-state assertion: fixed (hook, VO, caption, pill). 2 verse decoupled from the tap: fixed (attributed, slower, 13:28 dropped, truncated to the imperative, away from product sentence in primary text). 3 ring named aloud with its mechanism: fixed (L2). 4 B1: carried by "remember Allah often" and the Sunnah line; the full small-and-steady hadith is left to ad_01 by design. 5 words match the picture in C02: fixed (no promise of a ticking count). 6 first-frame story: the VO does not depend on the reach shot; Clip change C is the proper fix and is recommended, not assumed.
  • Lengths. Headlines 33, 27 and 37 characters. Description 25. Spoken words 26 (L1 6, L2 7, L3 6, L4 7; hyphenated compounds counted as one).
  • Overlap scan against the other rewrites and finals present (01-07, 09-11): no shared hook, headline or VO line. Deliberate shared elements: the price and guarantee wording (standard), "Tap to count" (product verb used across the batch), and the Sunnah idea (reworded). The closing VO ("Forty-nine ninety-nine. Free US shipping. Sixty-day money-back.") differs from ad_06 ("Sixty-day money-back.") and ad_07 ("Sixty-day money-back guarantee.") by leading with price and shipping.

Re-render needed

  • VO re-record: yes, all four lines are new. Re-time captions.ass using the table above after Whisper.
  • Clip re-render: none required. Two assembly pill patches are required before upload: C01 to "MORNING WALK" and C03 to "QURAN 62:10". One caption-layer pill is added in C02 ("ONE TAP, ONE COUNT").
  • Optional re-renders (recommended): C01 as a single-woman wrist-first POV with one tap; C02 with a visible thumb tap and a ticking count, ring on the ring finger.

Owner to confirm

  • No [CONFIRM] claims are used in this ad.
  • Open quran.com/62/10 once before running, as the offer brief asks for all verified lines.
  • Confirm the two pill patches (C01 and C03) are applied. The old "POV: your thoughts won't slow down" and "QURAN 13:28" pills must not ship with this VO.
  • Pronunciation of "istighfar", "Quran" and "Al Rayan" on the re-recorded VO.
  • Landing page, before this ad sends traffic: the rebuilt product.html and index.html still show "4.9 · 8,200+ wearers" / "Worn by 8,200+ Muslims" and a named quote ("Sarah W., New York"), which breaks the no-metrics, no-fabricated-testimonial rules. They also tell most people to wear the ring on the index finger, which conflicts with the men's-finger rule. Fix on the store, not in the ad.
  • Whether the owner wants a re-render of C02 so the ring is on the ring finger (see visual flags).
Critique of the previous version

ad_08 critique (video, POV overthinking walk, "Mind racing on your walk to work? Make it istighfar.")

Role: copy critic, not a rewrite. Reviewed: CONTEXT.md; intake; user market research; avatar sheet; master avatar research; necessary beliefs; offer brief; 03-ads/ad_08/brief.md, copy_final.md, chief_notes_v1.md, _assign.json and concept_slate.md entries; 04-assets/ad_08/script_display.txt, clip_args.txt, prompts/*, audio/vo_words.json (real Whisper timings), captions.ass; every start frame; a 1 fps contact sheet of the actual final.mp4 (13.6 s); the swipe analysis; the other 10 ads' copy_final.md; the rebuilt store pages.

Verdict in one line: the concept (a racing mind on a city walk, turned into istighfar) is a good A1 scene and the structure is clean. But the hook asserts a mental state about the viewer, the Quran line is placed so it reads as "tap the ring and your heart is at ease", the ring is never named aloud, B1 and B4 are not actually installed by any spoken line, and the picture does not show what the words say (no tap, no walking, no ticking count, two women in one coat). Faith wording and metrics are mostly clean. Most of the fixes are VO, caption and primary-text changes; three are clip changes, flagged below.

0. What actually shipped (anchor for everything below)

Final = 4 clips, 13.6 s, VO 13.28 s. Clip windows come from clip_args.txt: C01 0-3.5, C02 3.5-6.6, C03 (a2) 6.6-9.7, C04 (a2) 9.7-13.6.

Clip / window VO as spoken (Whisper) Burned caption (editable ASS) Baked-in pill (in the start frame, NOT editable without a clip change) What the picture shows (from the frames and the contact sheet)
C01 0-3.5 "Mind racing on your walk to work?" 0.00-1.72, gap, "Make it istighfar." 2.16-3.28 Mind racing on / your walk to work? / Make it istighfar POV: your thoughts won't slow down A POV arm in a camel coat reaches a palm toward the back of a second woman in a sage hijab and an identical camel coat, walking away. White scribbles circle the hand. The ring (5332) is on the hand. The arm swings and the ring hand drops back. No tap happens.

The hand stays raised and nearly still. The display reads 5332 and never changes. No visible tap, no visible dissolve of scribbles, no walking motion except background blur.

She stands facing camera, palm raised with scribbles fading beside her, then eyes closed and mouth slightly open at ~8 s (it reads as if she is reciting), then hand lowered. She is standing, not walking.

The hand does not tap. The background changes (a different street with parked cars) across the clip.

Words per second per line (VO): L1 10 words in 3.3 s = 3.0 w/s (the hook sentence itself is 7 words in 1.7 s); L2 7 words in 2.7 s = 2.6; L3 8 words in 2.5 s = 3.2 (fastest line, and it is a Quran verse); L4 8 words in 3.0 s = 2.6. Overall 33 words in 13.3 s = 2.5, but unevenly distributed, with a 0.7 s pause before the CTA.

1. Hook strength and ranking

First second as shipped. VO starts at 0.0 ("Mind racing on your walk to work?"), caption "Mind racing on" at 0.0, a green pill "POV: your thoughts won't slow down" on screen, and a hand with a ring reaching toward the back of a woman. Text arrives immediately and the scene is real, which is good. The problems:

  • Three layers say the same thing in the first 3.5 s: the pill ("your thoughts won't slow down"), the VO ("Mind racing"), and the caption ("Mind racing on"). The viewer learns nothing from the pill that the VO has not said. One of them should carry a different beat.
  • The "POV" label is false. A POV shot would be the speaker's eyes. Here the POV arm reaches toward another woman's back, as if about to tap a stranger on the shoulder. It reads as "someone is following her", not "my thoughts are racing". The scribbles around the hand are an abstract thought-loop, but the framing gives the eye a different story first.
  • Would an A1/A2 stop? A1 (a practising Muslim, 20-40, in a walking commute): probably a pause on "istighfar" at 2.2-3.3 s, because that is the in-group word, but it lands after the first two seconds. "Mind racing on your walk to work?" alone could be any meditation or ADHD ad. A2 (gift buyer): no signal at all; but this ad is A1, so that is fine.
  • Policy risk in the hook itself (see 3): "Mind racing?" plus "your thoughts won't slow down" asserts or implies a mental-health state about the viewer, and it is second-person negative framing.

Ranking of the six existing hooks (as spoken or overlay hooks for this clip set):

  1. H1 "Mind racing on your walk to work? Make it istighfar." Best of the six as built, because it names a scene and a turn in one line and "istighfar" is the tribe word. Weaknesses: mental-health-adjacent, "to work" narrows the scene (students, school run, lunch walk), and "Make it istighfar" is an imperative at the viewer's religious practice.
  2. H2 "My mind races the whole walk to work. So I tap." The strongest structure (confession plus action, 11 words) and the closest to the swipe's "I started wearing this because I would think a lot". But as first-person it reads as a customer or creator endorsement from an AI persona, and there are no verified Al Rayan customer quotes. It needs reframing into brand voice ("We hear this a lot: ...") or a clear second-person scenario.
  3. H5 "What if every racing thought became a chance for istighfar?" Good idea, but 11 words, "racing thought" again, and a question that asks the viewer to imagine rather than see.
  4. H6 "A steady rhythm of istighfar, even on the walk to work." Calm, on-brief (echoes Q39), but flat. It does not interrupt anything.
  5. H4 "Watch this walk: one tap, one istighfar, keep going." "Watch this walk" is empty, and the clips do not show one tap per istighfar (no tap is shown at all).
  6. H3 "You open your dhikr app and get pulled into social media." Wrong ad. This is ad_04's whole angle (phone trap, Q30). Drop it.

2. Angle and avatar fit; awareness and sophistication; belief order

  • Angle fit: sound. The A1 desire for "dhikr in dead moments" (avatar sheet, Short-Term Goals) and the commute-istighfar evidence (Q18, Q39, Q20) support this. Caveat: the VOC is a drive ("quiet drive between cities", "45-minute highway drive"), not a walk. The brief swapped to walking to avoid AI driving shots. That is fine for production, but the copy should not borrow the "1,000 in 20 minutes" claim. It correctly does not.
  • Distinct from the other 10 ads? Yes on scene and angle: this is the only walk and the only istighfar-led ad. The overlap is in the closing lines (see 5).
  • Awareness and sophistication. Problem-aware, stage 3. At stage 3 the lead should be the specific moment plus a usage mechanism. The ad has the moment but the mechanism is only "Tap. Keep walking. The count keeps up." The ring is never named or described aloud. At 13.6 s the viewer cannot tell what is being tapped.
  • Beliefs in order (B1 then B4): neither is actually installed by the script.
  • B1 = "steady dhikr through my day matters, and a small constant habit beats a big rare one." The chief notes say it is "installed by the callout premise (reader already values istighfar) and L3 (Quran 13:28)". That is not installation. Quran 13:28 is about tranquility, not about steadiness or smallness. There is no Bukhari 6464 or Tirmidhi 3375 line anywhere in the script.
  • B4 = "a ring can count silently, without a phone, noise or a look at a screen." The VO says nothing about silent, phone-free, or screen-free. "The count keeps up" is about keeping pace, not silence. The only B4 content is visual, and the visual does not show a tap or the count changing.
  • What the script actually installs is a third promise, "tap, and your heart is at ease", which is not on the belief list and is the riskiest of the three (see 3).
  • Missing proof beat. At stage 3 with a mechanism claim, one concrete fact would earn belief: the pulse at 33/66/99 (A-2), or "no click" (Q21). Neither is spoken.

3. Rule violations (quote the line)

3a. Faith accuracy and attribution

  • L3 VO "By the remembrance of Allah, hearts are assured." The verified wording (offer_brief row 1) is "...Unquestionably, by the remembrance of Allah hearts are assured". The VO drops "Unquestionably" and begins with a capital "By", so it is a clipped fragment. A fragment is acceptable only if it does not change the meaning. Here the meaning does shift, because the full verse is addressed to "those who have believed", and the clipped version is placed straight after "Tap. Keep walking. The count keeps up." That sequence plus the visual (her shoulders drop, her eyes close, scribbles vanish) reads as: tap the ring, then hearts are assured. CONTEXT.md: "Say what the dhikr earns, never what the ring earns." The sequencing breaks the spirit of that rule even though the words are the Quran's. Treat this as a MUST FIX.
  • No spoken attribution. The VO never says "Quran" or "Allah says"; only the baked-in pill "QURAN 13:28" does. A viewer with sound on and eyes elsewhere hears an unattributed quote. The pill itself is a bare citation; it shows no verse text, so the on-screen and spoken versions do not match each other (VO is the verse, pill is the reference, captions are a third chunking of the VO).
  • Primary text repeats the clipped line: "By the remembrance of Allah hearts are assured. (Quran 13:28)". Same issue; quote the full verified clause and place it away from the "tap" sentence.
  • Better fit available in the verified table: Quran 62:10 ("...disperse within the land... and remember Allah often that you may succeed") is literally about walking out into the day and remembering, and Quran 3:191 ("standing or sitting...") fits a walk. Muslim 2702 (the Prophet seeking forgiveness a hundred times a day) is the istighfar hadith that the angle is built on and the brief lists as an option, but the copy ignored it. Caution on 2702: it is 100, and the ring pulses at 33/66/99, so never imply the ring marks the 100th.
  • Never above finger counting / ring earns: not violated in words. "Make it istighfar" attributes the dhikr to the viewer, not the ring. OK.

3b. Meta personal-attributes policy (religion, health)

  • L1 "Mind racing on your walk to work?" and pill "POV: your thoughts won't slow down". Both describe a mental state of the viewer ("your thoughts"), in second person, in a negative-self-perception frame. Meta's personal-attributes rule covers implied mental-health conditions. "Mind racing / thoughts won't slow down" is anxiety-adjacent, and L3 then offers a Quran line that "assures hearts". The brief itself warned: "Avoid mental-health/anxiety cure claims". The shipped sequence (racing thoughts, tap, verse, shoulders drop) is exactly an anxiety-relief arc. MUST FIX. A situation-first hook ("the walk to work", "twelve quiet minutes", "the stretch between the train and the desk") avoids asserting anything about the viewer.
  • "Make it istighfar." Second-person imperative about the viewer's religious practice. CONTEXT.md calls this borderline and says prefer situation or product framing, or first-person brand voice. SHOULD FIX (or reframe to "Some walks are istighfar walks" style situation framing).
  • H2 "My mind races the whole walk to work. So I tap." (alternate hook, not shipped in the VO). If used, first-person plus an AI persona reads as a customer testimonial. There is no verified Al Rayan customer. Do not carry it into the rewrite without brand-voice or second-person reframing.

3c. Wudu fiqh, riya', men's finger, rose gold

  • Wudu: not mentioned. Fine.
  • Riya': no counts bragged. Fine. The ring shows 5332 on screen, which is the product image number, not a boast.
  • Men's finger / ring finger: the ad is about a woman, so there is no direct violation. But C02 shows the ring at the base of what looks like the index finger of the raised hand (prompt said ring finger). If a male viewer or a later male edit copies this visual, it models the index-finger wear the Muslim narration cautions against. Flag for a regenerate or a check on any reuse. The ring finger shot in C03 and C04 is fine.
  • Rose gold: not mentioned; black ring only. Fine.

3d. Fabricated testimonial risk

  • Shipped VO and on-image copy are second person; no first-person claim. Good.
  • Primary text: second person plus product description. Good.
  • Hook H2 (see 3b) is the only risk, and it is not in the render.
  • The persona is an AI-generated woman in photoreal UGC style. Beyond the copy, expect Meta's AI-content labelling. Not a copy fix, but do not write lines that imply she is a real customer ("I", "my").

3e. Unconfirmed or banned claims; store metrics

  • No store metrics. No stars, no 85% or 4.2x. Clean.
  • No weight, battery, IP68, "waterproof", "completely silent", "works without your phone", size exchange. Clean.
  • "The count keeps up" is vague but not a claim of automatic counting. Clean. If the rewrite says "the phone stays in your pocket", it needs [CONFIRM: works without your phone].
  • Primary text: "no loud clicker and no beads in your pocket" is fine as product framing, but it drifts into ad_05 and ad_09 territory (see 5).
  • Meta description "60-day money-back guarantee": fine.
  • No sale price: correct.

4. Copy-to-creative mismatch (what the picture shows vs what the words say)

Line What it says What the picture shows at that moment Mismatch
L1 "Mind racing on your walk to work?" A walk to work, a racing mind A hand reaching toward another woman's back on a sidewalk; scribbles around the hand; no visible commute cues (no bag strap for work, no building entry, no train). The "POV" pill is not a POV of the speaker. The scene reads as "following a stranger", not "my own thoughts". Two women in identical coats is confusing.
L2 "Tap." A tap A raised hand, ring up, still. No thumb tap. Medium to high. The one verb in the ad's mechanism is not shown.
L2 "Keep walking." Walking A raised hand held still with a blurred street. Medium. Motion is minimal.
L2 "The count keeps up." The count climbing Display reads 5332 the entire time. High. This is the retention beat of the ad and the digits never change.
L2 (prompt) "each tap makes one scribble loop dissolve" Scribbles dissolve Scribbles stay thin and faint; no obvious dissolve The visual metaphor (tap resolves the thought-loop) is lost.
L3 "By the remembrance of Allah, hearts are assured." Calm A different woman; eyes closed, mouth slightly open as if reciting, then standing still Identity drift between C02 and C03 (different face, different hijab shade, no bag). The "shoulders drop" beat is not visible. She is standing, not on a walk.
L4 "Sixty-day money-back guarantee. Tap Shop Now." Offer and CTA A raised palm like a "stop" gesture; the ring face is too small to read; the street changes. Pill "AL RAYAN DHIKR RING 2.0" is tiny caps. The product is not named in the VO and barely legible on screen. The gesture reads "stop" or "hi", not "tap".

On-image text quality:

  • Pill 1 "POV: your thoughts won't slow down": clean render and legible. Content issues in 3b. It sits over the first 3.5 s only.
  • Pill 2 "QURAN 13:28": clean render. Gives only a reference, not the verse text. Looks like a verified citation but gives the viewer nothing to read.
  • Pill 3 "AL RAYAN DHIKR RING 2.0": correct spelling, but it is the first and only time the product is named, in the last 3.9 s, at about 55% of the size of the other pills. Weak brand impression for a cold audience. This is the main reason the ring has to be named in VO.
  • Captions (ASS): word-group captions at about 65% of the frame height. Legible. They sit over the hand and ring in C02, and over her coat in C03/C04. They are editable.

5. Clarity, rhythm, flab, clichés, repetition, overlap with the other 10 ads

  • Rhythm. The short lines (L2 "Tap. Keep walking. The count keeps up.") are the best rhythm in the ad. L1 is rushed (hook sentence in 1.7 s). L3 is 8 words in 2.5 s: an eight-word verse fragment read at 3.2 w/s with a pause before "hearts" is too fast for a Quran line, and it is the emotional peak.
  • Flab and redundancy. "Mind racing... thoughts won't slow down" is said twice in 3.5 s. The caption repeats the VO and the pill repeats both. L4 "Sixty-day money-back guarantee. Tap Shop Now." is serviceable, but "Tap" is now both the product verb (L2) and the button verb (L4). The word is overloaded.
  • Cliché. "POV: ..." is the swipe's device and is fine for a UGC walk, but "Mind racing" is the category cliché of meditation and ADHD ads. In an Islamic ad the freshest thing is the specific dhikr word (istighfar), which currently lands late.
  • Missing. No product name aloud, no price, no free shipping, no pulse, no "silent". The viewer finishes the ad not knowing what the object is, how much, or why it is better than the beads in their pocket.
  • Overlap with other ads:
  • Closing line: ad_06 "Sixty days, money back. Tap Shop Now.", ad_07 "Sixty days to try it. Money back." (overlay TAP SHOP NOW), ad_08 "Sixty-day money-back guarantee. Tap Shop Now." Three video ads end on near-identical CTAs. Differentiate or at least vary the offer beat.
  • "Tap. ... Tap." phrasing appears in ad_01, ad_04, ad_05, ad_06, ad_07, ad_09, ad_10 as "Tap to count". Fine as product language, but here it is also the VO's second sentence and the CTA's first word.
  • Primary text line "no loud clicker and no beads in your pocket" overlaps ad_05 and ad_09 ("Beads scatter. Clickers shout.", "Retire the loud clicker"). Cut it; this ad does not need the us-vs-them.
  • Quran 13:28 and istighfar are used in no other ad. Good, keep them exclusive to ad_08. Quran 2:152 is ad_04's; Tirmidhi 3375 is ad_06 and ad_11; Bukhari 6464 is ad_01 and ad_07; Muslim 596a is ad_07. So the unused verified lines available to ad_08 without overlap are 13:28, 62:10, 3:191, 33:41 and Muslim 2702.
  • "The walk" and "commute" are unique to this ad (only ad_10 mentions walking, in passing).

6. Script timing (words per line vs clip durations)

Line VO window Clip window Words / seconds / w/s Fit
L1 0.00-3.28 C01 0-3.5 10 / 3.3 / 3.0 Fits but rushed; "Mind racing on your walk to work?" is 1.7 s
L2 3.62-6.30 C02 3.5-6.6 7 / 2.7 / 2.6 Good. The best-paced line
L3 6.84-9.30 C03 6.6-9.7 8 / 2.5 / 3.2 Too fast for a verse; ends 0.4 s before the clip ends and is followed by 0.7 s of dead air
L4 10.02-13.06 C04 9.7-13.6 8 / 3.0 / 2.6 OK. Starts 0.3 s into the clip; no space to name the ring
  • Total 33 words in 13.28 s vs the 15 s target and the 13.6 s render: the ad is 1.4 s shorter than the brief, so there is no timing pressure to squeeze words. There is room for a slightly fuller L4 and a slower L3.
  • Overlay text is only on-image in three baked pills (not editable) and the captions (editable). The Meta-spec "overlay ≤6 words" is met by the pills (5, 2, 5 words), but see the redundancy in 4.
  • Line to clip match. The structure maps well: hook, tap, release, CTA. The mismatch is inside each clip (no tap, no count change, standing instead of walking), not the timing.
  • Practical limits for a VO-only rewrite (at 2.3-2.5 w/s, VO starts at 0.0 and must end by ~13.2 s): C01 about 7-8 words, C02 about 6-7, C03 about 6-7 (slower, for the verse), C04 about 8-9. Total about 30-32.

MUST FIX

  1. Remove the mental-state assertion. Replace "Mind racing on your walk to work?" (VO + caption) and keep the baked pill "POV: your thoughts won't slow down" from carrying meaning alone. The pill cannot change without a clip change (C01 start frame), so either (a) write a VO that does not repeat it and lets the pill be a visual label, or (b) re-render C01 with a new pill. Situation framing ("the walk to work", "the stretch between the train and the desk") or a brand-voice line, not "your mind / your thoughts".
  2. Decouple the verse from the tap. As built, "Tap. Keep walking. The count keeps up. By the remembrance of Allah, hearts are assured." plus the relieved visual says "the ring brings tranquility". Put the verse in a position and phrasing that credits the dhikr (e.g. "Allah's own words on remembrance"), use the full verified clause with "Unquestionably", say who said it, or swap to Quran 62:10 / 3:191 / 33:41, which fit a walk and do not promise a feeling. Also slow it to at most 6-7 words in ~3 s.
  3. Name the ring aloud, and say what it does. Right now the product is never in the VO. One concrete mechanism line (tap, count on your finger, gentle pulse at 33, 66, 99) must be spoken, not only captioned. This also installs B4 for real.
  4. Install B1 for real or drop it from the brief. One sentence of small-and-steady (Bukhari 6464, attributed correctly as "The Prophet ﷺ said (narrated by Aisha)", or Muslim 2702) is better than an unlinked verse. If 13:28 stays, B1 is not installed and the brief should say the ad carries B4 and a faith anchor only.
  5. Match the words to the picture for L2. "Tap." and "The count keeps up." have no visible support. Either (a) clip change: re-render C02 with a visible thumb tap and digits ticking (e.g. 0033 rolling toward the pulse), or (b) rewrite L2 to describe what C02 does show (a ring on a raised hand, a still display) and not promise motion.
  6. Fix the first-frame story. The hand reaching toward another woman's back reads as "touching a stranger". Clip change (C01): single woman, wrist-first POV from her own eyes, hand at hip, one tap. If no re-render is possible, write the opening VO so it does not say "POV" or "your" and treat the figure ahead as "the walk".

SHOULD FIX

  1. Change "Make it istighfar" from imperative at the viewer's practice to situation or product framing.
  2. Different CTA from ad_06 and ad_07: vary the offer beat (add $49.99 and free US shipping; "sixty days to try it" is ad_07's line).
  3. Pace: hook sentence under 2.5 w/s; verse under 2.5 w/s; use the 1.4 s of spare runtime and the 0.7 s dead air before L4.
  4. Primary text: cut "no loud clicker and no beads in your pocket" (ad_05/09 territory), put the verse in full with correct attribution and not directly after "Tap the ring", and keep one CTA. Currently 6 short paragraphs; trim to 4.
  5. Pill/caption redundancy in the first 3.5 s: give the captions different words than the pill, or kill the caption in C01.
  6. C03/C04 identity drift (different woman, no bag, hijab shade) and the "stop-hand" gesture in C04. Flag for a clip change if budget allows; otherwise accept and avoid copy that depends on continuity ("she", "my").
  7. Check the ring finger in C02 (looks like index). For any reuse, avoid it.
  8. If a first-person hook is wanted (H2), reframe as brand voice ("We hear this a lot"). Do not voice "I" from the AI persona.

KEEP (what works)

  • The scene: a short city walk, morning light, the ring on the hand. Real, specific, and unique among the 11 ads.
  • Istighfar as the dhikr: unique to this ad and the right word for the tribe.
  • L2's rhythm ("Tap. Keep walking. The count keeps up.") as a pattern: three short beats, 2.6 w/s.
  • Structure of the swipe (hook, mechanism, release, CTA) mapping cleanly to four clips at the right durations; VO-to-clip timing is actually well aligned.
  • Single CTA, no store metrics, no sale price, no weight or battery claims, no competitor jabs.
  • Quran 13:28 as an exclusive faith anchor for this ad (no other ad uses it), if reframed per MUST FIX 2.
  • Word-by-word captions in a legible bold style (editable).

Directions for the rewrite (3-5)

  1. Situation hook, not state hook. Lead with the scene and the word istighfar inside the first 1.5 s, with no "you" + mental state. For example "The walk to work. Twelve minutes of istighfar waiting to happen." (check "twelve minutes" reads as a scenario, not a claim; or "Between the train and the desk, there's a dhikr in every step."). Keep it at most 7-8 words for C01.
  2. Give the ring a name and a verb in the middle. C02 window (3.1 s, ~6-7 words): the product and the mechanism in one breath, e.g. "Al Rayan Dhikr Ring. One tap, one count." If the tap is not visible, either re-render C02 or phrase it as what the picture shows ("Your count, on your finger").
  3. Faith line that earns, then credits. Use Quran 62:10 (disperse in the land, remember Allah often) or 33:41 for the walk, or Muslim 2702 with correct attribution. Say who said it, keep it to a slow ~6 words in ~3 s, and do not place it directly after "tap". If 13:28 stays, say "Allah says" / use "Unquestionably", and make the visual calm come from the walk, not from the tap.
  4. Make the close different from ad_06 and ad_07. C04 has 3.9 s: "Al Rayan Dhikr Ring 2.0. $49.99. Sixty days to try it." or a variant with free US shipping, with the CTA word "Shop Now" only in the caption and button, not "Tap Shop Now" again.
  5. Install B1 once, in primary text if not in VO. Primary text can carry "small and steady" with Bukhari 6464 properly attributed, and one line on counting on fingers remaining the Sunnah. This keeps the VO short and the faith framing complete without crowding a 13.6 s video.

Editor notes: changes, checks, re-render, owner to confirm

Editor notes

What I changed from the rewrite, and why

  1. Opener swapped. Line 1 is now the scene ("The meeting goes quiet. The clicker doesn't.") and the trade-up line ("It did its job. Time for the drawer.") moved to line 2, with a shortened wording. The scene is the specific, visual moment and the better scroll-stop; the trade-up line works as the follow-up that gives the image headline its dignified reading. Both stay in the hook table.
  2. Sunnah line replaced. The rewrite's "The ring is for the long counts and the busy hands" is now the formula in ad_04, ad_05, ad_08, ad_10 and ad_11 (ad_01, ad_06 and ad_07 already reworded theirs). New line: "Fingers remain the Sunnah. Clicker or ring, it's only an aid for the count." It is unique in the batch, treats the clicker and the ring the same way (no ring-above-fingers reading, matches the "retire without insult" tone) and makes no scholar-endorsement claim. Hook H5 matches.
  3. Risk-reversal paragraph rewritten. The rewrite's "Try it for 60 days. Miss the click? You're covered..." clashed with the batch ("Try it for 60 days" is used by ad_02, ad_05, ad_11; "Sixty days to decide" by ad_07) and made the guarantee read as conditional on missing the click. Now: "Keep the clicker in the drawer for now. If the ring isn't for you, you're covered by our 60-day money-back guarantee." It pays off the drawer image, stays unconditional and uses the exact guarantee wording.
  4. Mechanism line. "One press of your thumb" became "Tap to count" (the allowed-claim wording; the thumb detail added an unneeded fiqh/finger implication). Added "where you are" for the display and kept the 33/66/99 line exactly, which does not imply the 100th.
  5. Product named earlier in structure. "Meet the Al Rayan Dhikr Ring 2.0" now opens the third paragraph; the name lands at about character 100.
  6. Meta headline 3 was "Silent tracking. Pulse at 33, 66, 99." (ad_07 owns the pulse hook; ad_11's headline is close). Now "Smart tasbih ring. 1-year tech warranty." (40 characters, at the cap). Headline 1 and 2 kept.
  7. Meta description "Free US shipping. 1-year tech warranty on every ring." duplicated ad_03's description and the primary text. Now "Black or rose gold. Free US shipping." (colours are an allowed claim; adds information).
  8. Hook H6 changed from "$49.99. 60 days to decide. 1-year tech warranty." to the plain offer stack (avoids ad_07's "Sixty days to decide").
  9. Re-render status softened to "recommended, not required", with the fallback copy confirmed to work.

MUST FIX check (critique)

  1. Finger-counting honoured in one line (Sunnah line). Resolved.
  2. No "your dhikr" or viewer-religion assertion anywhere in hooks or text. Resolved (only the old on-image subhead, in the fallback, still says "your dhikr"; the recommended re-render removes it).
  3. Repetition cut: "Retire the loud clicker" appears once (image). Line 1 does not restate the headline. CTA line and all Meta headlines add information. Resolved.
  4. Single villain: no beads, no phone, no "cheap counter that quit". Durability is one warranty sentence. Resolved.
  5. VOC-21 lift removed. Resolved.

Hard-rule re-check

  • No Quran or hadith quoted, so no attribution risk. Finger counting is referenced only as "remain the Sunnah" (Abu Dawud 1501 supports it; no quote used).
  • No ring reward, iman or khushu' claims; never above finger counting; no wudu, riya', rose-gold-as-gold or men's-index-finger content. No hand is shown on the image and no finger instruction is given.
  • No testimonial, first-person story, store metrics, stars, superlatives, competitor names, sale price, % off, delivery speed, IP68, "waterproof", weight or battery claims. "Silent tracking" (not "completely silent") is used.
  • Not used: works-without-phone, free size exchanges, battery, app-as-store-listing. No [CONFIRM] tags are needed.
  • Counts: Meta headlines 36 / 37 / 40; description 37; primary text 94 words; on-image 17 words.

Re-render needed

Recommended, text layer only: SUBHEAD to "A dhikr ring with silent tracking."; offer bar to "$49.99 + FREE US SHIPPING". Optional creative note: give the black ring a subtle rim light or a slightly larger size, because on the near-black ground it is lower contrast than the scuffed clicker. No clip changes (image ad).

Owner to confirm

  1. The side button is quiet. The ad is built on the ring being quieter than a clicker. "Silent tracking" and "no clicking sounds in meetings" are brand wording, untested by us. If the button makes an audible click in a quiet room, change "Silent tracking." and the subhead to "quiet".
  2. Offer stack: $49.99, free US shipping, 60-day money-back guarantee, 1-year tech warranty. Do not add the 25% off or $22.65 sale price until confirmed live. Also confirm the 60-day return process is as simple as the copy implies ("covered by our 60-day money-back guarantee"); "free returns" is deliberately not claimed.
  3. Landing page (unpublished rebuild at alrayan-rebuild/html/product.html and index.html), checked in this pass and still present: a named testimonial ("Sarah W., New York"), "Loved by 8,200+ Muslim wearers" and named review cards (fake testimonial and store-metric rules); sizes listed as 18, 20 and 22 mm only (not 16-22 mm); "Do I need my phone with me? No." in the FAQ (unconfirmed); and "Most people wear it on the index finger" in the sizing and FAQ copy (conflicts with the Sahih Muslim caution for men). Fix before the traffic arrives.
  4. Message match: the page's comparison table says "Every tool has its place", which is gentler than "RETIRE". This copy keeps a clicker-friendly tone ("did its job", "only an aid") so the click-through does not feel like a bait-and-switch.

Overlap check

Other ads' finals/rewrites checked (ad_01-08, 10, 11). No shared hook or Meta headline. "Fingers remain the Sunnah..." and "Keep the clicker in the drawer" are unique; the "long counts and busy hands" formula and "Try it for 60 days" / "Sixty days to decide" are deliberately not used. ad_03's note about this ad's old "60 days money back" headline is respected: ad_03 leads with the size fear, and this ad leads with the clicker trade-up.

Critique of the previous version

ad_09 copy critique ("RETIRE THE LOUD CLICKER", Carbinox swipe, image 9:16, black ring)

Critic: copy chief, with Islamic adab and Meta policy checks. Judged against 03-ads/ad_09/copy_final.md, report_media/ad_09_main.jpg (viewed), CONTEXT.md hard rules, the research stack, and the other 10 ads' copy_final.md.
